1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

羅子評 
(886-2)2563-1666 
jason.luo@firstsec.com.tw

豐興(2015) 
區間操作, TP 68 (潛在報酬 6.58%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/31)：63.8 
Target Price：68

豐興 26Q2 雖營收年減 9.20% 至 75.87 億元，但受惠型鋼與條
線出貨成長及產品組合優化，毛利率攀升至 12.74%；加計業外認
列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻 ， 稅後淨利達 8.47 億元 ，YoY 26.61%，
EPS 為 1.45 元。  展望 2026 全年，受鋼筋出貨承壓影響，預
估營收為 272.14 億元 ，YoY -6.28%； 但在型鋼與條線出貨維穩，
以及第二季業外收益的挹注下 ， 預估全年稅後淨利將成長至 25.31 
億元，YoY 12.28%，預估全年 EPS 為 4.35 元。   
評價方面 ， 按照過往 75% 配息率估算 ，2027 年有望配發 3.2 元
現金股利， 隱含殖利率約 5.1%。 考量獲利雖優於 2025 年且具備
殖利率支撐 ， 但未來整體營運動能受限 ， 故給予歷史本淨比中緣位
階 1.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設定為 68 元，投資建議為區間操
作。 
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
豐興鋼鐵(2015)成立於 1969 年，為台灣主要電爐鋼廠之一，
也是國內唯一同時具備型鋼 、 條線及鋼筋三大產品線的煉軋合
一鋼鐵廠。 
產品組合方面，鋼筋約佔營收 54%，市占率約 14.4%、條線
佔 25%、型鋼佔 21%。產能配置方面，豐興擁有85 噸 DC 電
弧爐和 100 噸 AC 電弧爐各一座 ， 搭配三條直列式軋鋼設備，
年產能達 180 萬噸。公司採用垂直整合的生產模式，具備完
整的煉鋼到軋鋼生產線，不僅可自給自足鋼胚，更能有效控制
生產成本。在原料採購策略上，豐興採取分批小量採購的方
式，有效降低原料價格波動風險。主要原料廢鐵和鋼胚約占成
本的六到七成，

⚫ 鋼筋銷售下滑，營收年減 9.20%至 75.87 億，EPS 1.45 元 
26Q2 鋼筋銷售年減 2 成，型鋼出貨與條線出貨年增，合併營
收 75.87 億，QoQ 18.47% ，YoY -9.20%；獲利受出貨季增
且產品組合優化 ， 毛利率升至 12.74%， 季增 1.20 個百分點，
年增 1.33 個百分點；營業利益 7.34 億，QoQ 24.47%，YoY 
-0.90%；業外認列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻，稅後獲利攀升至  
8.47 億，QoQ 60.83%，YoY 26.61%；EPS 為 1.45 元。

型鋼需求表現堅挺，預期全年出貨呈現持平至小幅成長，條線
目前雖處庫存消化階段，且下游需求呈分化 （螺帽與機械零組
件相對穩健，螺絲與手工具偏保守） ，但兩項產品展現出強韌
的定價能力；在原料跌價時報價依然穩定，價格與利差具備實
質支撐，預期將成為後市營收與獲利的核心動能。   
綜合評估，在鋼筋出貨承壓、型鋼與條線獲利護航的結構下，
預估全年營收為 272.14 億元，YoY -6.28%。受惠於產品組
合優化，預估毛利率將提升至  12.71%，年增 1.18 個百分
點，營業利益達 25.91 億元，YoY 3.54% 。加計第二季轉投
資等業外收益的顯著挹注，預估稅後淨利將攀升至 25.31 億
元，YoY 12.28%， 全年 EPS 為 4.35 元 ， 每股淨值約 39.91 
元。

股價相對大盤走勢

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

中長期業績展望佳，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年， AI 基礎設施的需求正帶動整體市場
復甦，並外溢至記憶體、網通與電源管理領域，然消費性電子
(如手機、 PC) 終端需求仍偏弱，且庫存天數有微幅上升的跡象。
儘管如此，聯電預期其 2026 年整體晶圓出貨量仍將成長，特別
是在 22/28 nm 與 8 吋的市占率持續提升。聯電預估 2026 年 AI
相關營收將近 3 億美元，並預期 3 年內突破 10 億美元大關。預
估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.72 元。公司在先進封裝 (interposer 或
矽電容等 )、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取得進展，為 2027
年後中長期成長奠定基礎。雖聯電 2Q26 獲利大多以投資收益
為主， 3Q26 股市成長動能相對弱，可能壓抑業外收益，但本業
獲利逐步回升，建議 Buy ，目標價 169 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.36 元：聯電 2Q26 營收為 687.33 億元，
QoQ+12.61% ，整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓 112.90 萬片，
QoQ+10.58% 。 2Q26 產能利用率增加至  85% 。 ASP 穩定向上。
2Q26 產能利用率增至 85% ， ASP QoQ 小增。 2Q26 毛利率增加
3.28 個百分點至 32.48% 。業外認列  302.36 億元收益，包含利
息收益  2.18 億元、匯兌收益 0.03 億元，認列投資收益  300.45 
億元 (1Q26 則為  49.97 億元投資收益 )。聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.36 元 。 若不計算投資收益 ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.97 元，QoQ+9.31% 。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元：預期 3Q26 在電腦、通訊及消費
性領域的需求動能將保持穩定。出貨成長主要由電源管理  IC 、
感測器及微控制器（ MCU ）的強勁需求所推動。 8 吋晶圓產品
線正迎來強勁反彈，預期 3Q26 產能利用率將有顯著改善；而  
12 吋產能目前已處於健康的利用率水位，足以支撐核心業務發
展。預 估 聯 電3Q26 晶圓出貨量 QoQ+7~9% ， 產能利用率約 90%。
ASP in USD QoQ+0% ，不過 2026 年的定價環境更具優勢。這主
要源於市場供需關係的結構性改善，毛利率增至約 35% 左右。
預估聯電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

全新 (2455 TT)  Buy

2H26 、 2027 年營收動能可期，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：07/2026 全新營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史
新高，主要係 AI data center 相關需求持續熱絡，且出貨表現亦
隨磷化銦基板料況改善而回歸常軌所致，且 2H26、2027 年營收
動能仍可期，故群益對全新的投資建議為 Buy 。  
07/2026 營收創新高： 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元， QoQ+0.63% ，其中，
微電子營收 7.04 億元， QoQ-1.89% ，與 1Q26 相去有限；光電子
產品營收 2.61 億元， QoQ+8.12% ，但 01~06/2026 僅 02/2026 受
農曆春節影響而僅 0.68 億元，其餘各月份光電子營收均為
0.86~0.88 億元，換言之，缺料問題仍影響光電子產品出貨表現。  
不過， 07/2026 營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史新高，關
鍵在於磷化銦基板缺料狀況將隨中國政府 05/2026 、 07/2026 兩
度放行管制而改善 ， 帶動全新光電子產品營收增加至 1.69 億元 ，
MoM+93.74% ；至於微電子產品營收 2.53 億元，亦為近幾年相
對高水準。  
2H26 展望優於 1H26：雖然 AI data center 相關需求熱絡，然而，
01~06/2026 光電子產品月營收成長性仍受制於磷化銦基板缺
料。全新磷化銦基板過去多數由美國業者的中國廠供應，然而，
受到中國政府管制磷化銦基板出口數量影響， 4Q25 全新光電子
產品營收逐月減少，且全新來自該供應商的磷化銦基板庫存
12/2025 已陸續用罄， 01~06/2026 磷化銦基板主要由第 2、 3 供
應商供應。  
所幸，中國政府 05/2026 月底批准 2026 年第 1 批磷化銦基板出
口， 07/2026 則再通過第 2 批，且總數量大於第 1 批，第 1、 2
批合計數量更已超過 2025 年總數量，此外，全新 06/2026 已陸
續接獲第 1 批獲准出口的磷化銦基板並用以生產光電子產品，
2H26 展望優於 1H26 。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標而言，由於全新專注於製造、銷售及研發化
合物半導體磊晶片，溫室氣體排放量多寡，主要和能源耗用、產能及產品組
成有關。全新乃就單位產品的能源耗用與溫室氣體排放量，設定目標為每年
減少 5%，並將視產能擴充與產品變化情形，賡續追蹤與調整設定與目標。
至於企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果的部份，全新為客戶代
工生產的產品多數運用於通訊等電子產品，非屬終端消費性產品；全新除協
助客戶設計低耗能、高效率產品，以降低產品生命週期內的資源耗用外，亦
持續進行生產製造過程的節能與減廢，以降低對環境所造成的衝擊，減少單
位產品所消耗的資源與污染物，為客戶及社會創造減碳環保效益。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

M
Update
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Credo's
FY1Q27 Results
We see positive implications for Bizlink from Credo's comments
on expanding AEC   and emerging ALC opportunities.
FY1Q27 (ended July 2026) results:
• Credo (CRDO.US, not covered) reported revenue of US$479mn (+10% QoQ/
+115% YoY), roughly in line with the consensus of US$474mn and guidance
of US$465-475mn.
• Non-GAAP GM was 68% (-0.3ppt QoQ) in line with consensus at 68% and

guidance at 67-69%.
• Non-GAAP NP was US$236mn (+4% QoQ/+140% YoY), roughly in line with
consensus at US$232mn.
• Four customers each contributed more than 10% of Credo's revenue in
FY1Q27, i.e., 33%, 28%, 13% and 10%, respectively.
FY2Q27 and FY27 outlook:
•  FY2Q27 revenue is guided to be US$525-535mn (+11% QoQ/+98% YoY at the
midpoint, largely in line with consensus of US$526mn), non-GAAP GM of 67-
69% (vs. consensus at 67.5%) and opex of US$100-105mn.

• For FY27, Credo now expects total revenue to grow >85% YoY (vs.
consensus at ~86% YoY), with an  inflection in 2H mainly from optical
revenue (>US$600mn for FY27 from Zero-Flap Optics, silicon photonics PICs
and optical DSPs, with each to contribute >US$100mn).
• Credo expects its FY27 GM to stay flat YoY, while operating expenses might
increase by ~55% YoY.
• 1.6T AEC might start to emerge from 2HFY27.
• ALCs and OmniConnect gearboxes remain on track for initial revenue from

FY28, management believes both are multi-billion dollar opportunities for
Credo.
• It also sees increasing customer activity on NPO for scale up network, with
confirmed PIC design wins for ramp in FY28.
Bizlink Read-across:
• We see positive implications from Credo's comments on the AEC's spec
upgrade and its penetration into more hyperscalers and neoclouds, as well as
the reiterated timing of ALC ramp in FY28.
• We stay constructive on Bizlink for its multiple drivers in the AI deployment

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+54%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Bizlink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales came in at NT$8,892mn (-8% MoM/+54% YoY)
• QTD reached 65% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$28,521mn (+23% QoQ/+55%
YoY) and consensus at NT$27,268mn (+17% QoQ/+48% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 revenue is tracking roughly in line with our estimate.
• Looking into 2H26, we expect HPC to remain the main growth driver, as

power interconnects should continue to see momentum from AI/general
servers. Meanwhile, data interconnects should grow along with new ASIC
model ramp and GPU sever demand.
• Moreover, semi cap equipment revenue growth should also sustain.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Bizlink (3665.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an
earnings CAGR of 46% seen over 2025-28e, which implies a PEG of around 0.7. This is in line
with the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at an average 13x P/E for a 19%
earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Faster and wider AEC adoption
n Data center and semi equipment capex increases that drive stronger HPC and semi-cap

business
n Stronger recovery for automotive business
Risks to Downside
n Lower-than-expected AEC adoption
n Intensifying competition
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower-than-expected growth in the semi capital equipment market
n Continued weakness in the auto industry

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
07 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the expansion of its addressable market.
Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the
company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment, rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing

value of thermal content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural drivers still in place, we expect
the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported by other
qualitative factors such as cold-plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2)

heightened price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling
technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

EV business in August 2026: Hon Hai’s EV subsidiary Foxtron (2258.TW, not covered)

(read more in our report) and zero emission buses (read more on the company’s 
announcement) suggests scope for incremental growth opportunities for Foxtron which 
could be positive for Hon Hai.

earnings by 1%, mainly on lower revenues; meanwhile we remain positive on gradual AI 
server ramp up and smartphones spec upgrade to drive growth ahead. We keep 2027E/ 
2028E estimates largely unchanged.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

ramp-up of D2C satellites from 2H27, with PCB area consumption to further increase by
40-50%, and (3) the ramp-up of AI satellites from 2H27, with PCB dollar content value
that is similar to LEO satellites, but at a significantly larger annual deployment volume of
several tens of thousands of units (vs. 3-4K LEO satellites launched by the key LEO
customer in 2025).
(6) The company mentioned that it is seeing some impact from material shortages, but

primarily constrained by E-glass. However, management mentioned there are no supply
issues for high-end CCL, while supply is well-supported by key CCL suppliers. In terms of
rising material costs, the company said it can pass through cost increase across nearly all
products, except for SLP products for the key smartphone customer.
(7) The company said it is seeing multiple growth opportunities for its data center PCB

business, including (1) strong demand for high-end PCB for AI satellites from its key LEO
customers (as mentioned in bullet point 4), (2) PCB for AI servers from the key
smartphone customer, which is expected to ramp-up from 4Q26, (3) additional
contributions from China customers with PCB demand across switches and AI servers,
and (4) an ongoing project with a small-scale ASIC client.
PCB/CCL sector read-across
We retain our positive view on the optical transceivers’ mSAP demand outlook, while our

supply chain checks recently suggested mSAP as a key bottleneck for the 1.6T optical
transceivers supply chain now, whose product pricing has gone up by 30%+ YTD with
good profitability (our checks and Compeq’s comments suggest the GM for 1.6T optical
transceivers mSAP are generally 40-45%+). We believe ZDT has the best opportunity to
gain more market share in this high-growth and profitable market (we expect TAM for
1.6T+ optical transceivers mSAP to go up from US$0.8bn in 2026 to US$2.9bn in 2028,

implying 94% CAGR), considering it has the most aggressive mSAP capacity expansion
plan (we expect ZDT to expand mSAP capacity by 5x in the next two years) and the
company’s best-in-class BT/mSAP production technology, which could become the
company’s key revenue driver (we expect mSAP to account for ~8%/10%+ of ZDT
revenue in 2027/28 from ~2% in 2026) and margin driver (mSAP GM should be 40-45%+
vs. ZDT 1H26 GM at only ~22+%).
We also expect CCL suppliers, such as EMC and TUC, to benefit from the strong optical

transceiver demand, and we expect both companies to gain more share with more CCL
capacity to be ready in 2027/28 (see here and here).
Overall, we retain our positive view on ZDT’s business outlook not only on mSAP
products, but also seeing a significant margin expansion opportunity from the high-end
AI PCB and ABF substrate products, where we also see a strong demand outlook (see our
AI PCB industry report here and our ABF substrate industry report here).

We maintain our Buy ratings on ZDT, EMC and TUC with unchanged 12m TPs of
NT$925/NT$10,200/NT$3,010.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$925 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line with industry downcycle 
average valuation).  
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market. 
Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
7 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan PCB/CCLFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

## Document 107: 260909_daiwa_EPM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_EPM.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM) reported its August 2026 
revenue after market hours on 9 September 2026.

What’s the impact: August revenue beat expectations. EPM reported 
August 2026 revenue of TWD359.8m, up 95.7% MoM and 111.5% YoY. 
QTD revenue reached 88.4% of our previous 3Q26 forecast and 74.2% of 
the Bloomberg consensus. Given the faster-than-expected revenue 
recognition schedule and a sharp increase in recognised high-end 
equipment units, which should drive a more favourable product mix, we 
raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 33% to TWD972.7m, gross margin by 
3.1ppts to 48.5%, and EPS by 92% to TWD1.86.

Vacuum laminators are emerging as the next ABF bottleneck. Despite 
a partial conversion of backlog into revenue in August, EPM’s backlog 
continued to increase from TWD810m at end-July to TWD880m in early 
September. With lead times extending further to 36 months, vacuum 
laminators are emerging as the next bottleneck in the ABF capacity 
expansion cycle. Given the robust demand from ABF substrate makers, we 
see upside potential to EPM’s capacity expansion plans. On margins, we 
expect continued product-mix improvement and the full realisation of price 
increases to lift gross margin by 6.6ppts/1.3ppts YoY to 53.2%/54.5% in 
2027/28E, driving a 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 141%.

Moulding film adoption opens a new growth avenue. Traditional 
transfer and compression moulding typically use granular, powder, or liquid-
type compounds for encapsulation. During our visit to SEMICON, Japanese 
material suppliers Nagase (8012 JP, JPY1,150.5, Outperform [2]; covered 
by Masayuki Nagano) and Resonac (4004 JP, JPY16,070, Outperform [2]; 
covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) noted growing customer interest in 
moulding films. These offer better filling performance, better warpage 
control and higher flowability. This makes them increasingly suitable for 
advanced packaging applications such as panel level packaging and 
component-embedded substrates. EPM supplies moulding-film laminators 
for both applications, with revenue contribution expected to begin in 2027.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 22-26% to reflect the 
full realisation of price increases and improving product mix. We reaffirm 
our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12-month TP to TWD720 (from TWD600) 
based on an unchanged target PER of 40x, applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: slower-than-expected substrate capex 
growth.

How we differ: We likely conduct a more comprehensive analysis than 
others on ABF industry demand and moulding film development.

9 September 2026

Vacuum laminators: the next ABF bottleneck

➢ 3Q26 QTD revenue ahead on higher high-end equipment recognition 
➢ Product mix and price hikes drive margins to new highs 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating; lifting 12M TP to TWD720

Company profile 
Eternal was established in 2022 through the spin-off of Eternal Material’s equipment division and its 
subsequent merger with Nikko-Materials, a wholly owned Japanese subsidiary. The company was listed 
in January 2026 and is the global leader in IC substrate vacuum laminators, serving all major substrate 
manufacturers worldwide.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 26 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Eternal demonstrates a solid corporate governance structure supported by a balanced and
diversified board composition. The company’sboard consists of 7 members, including 3
independent directors, with backgrounds spanning industry operations, finance, accounting,
legal, and academia, enabling comprehensive oversight across business and governance
functions.
Capital management 2
Eternal adopts a prudent capital management strategy, supported by a stable shareholder
structure and disciplined financial policy. The company is majority-owned by Eternal Materials
(over 60% stake), providing long-term strategic backing and financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Eternal discloses related-party transactions transparently and enforces strict governance
procedures to manage potential conflicts of interest. Board members are required to recuse
themselves from discussions and voting on matters involving personal or affiliated interests,
such as asset acquisitions, patent licensing, and remuneration decisions.
S Product quality &
safety 1
Eternal maintains strict product quality control, covering design, manufacturing, and delivery
processes. The company emphasizes equipment reliability and performance stability, supported
by long-term client relationships and low defect risks, ensuring high customer satisfaction and
consistent product safety standards.
E Product design &
lifecycle management 2
Eternal focuses on enhancing product durability and energy efficiency through continuous R&D
innovation. Its equipment design emphasizes precision, extended lifecycle and reduced material
waste, aligning product development with resource efficiency and long-term environmental
sustainability goals.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
GB200 and GB300 NVL72
Racks in August 2026
We provide monthly/quarterly rack shipment forecasts for
ODMs. Within the major Server ODMs, our order of preference
is Wistron  > Hon Hai > Quanta, based on upside to PT.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Our CY26 full-year forecast is now at 80-85K racks: This year is a strong one for
downstream rack assembly. We expect over 100% y/y growth in rack shipments, vs.

~26.5k last year.
Quanta (2382.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$424.0B (+16% m/m, +177% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at 2.5-
2.55K racks (+22% m/m), despite offset slightly by lower NB shipments at 2.6M
units (-7% m/m, -37% y/y). Combining July and August GB200/300 rack shipments,
Quanta has already shipped ~4.6K racks, which would imply upside to our current
3Q forecast of ~6.4K racks. We think there is likely also upside to consensus

forecast, since combined July+August revenue reached ~NT$790B, which is 81% of
MSe and 71% of consensus 3Q revenue estimates. This also implies that there could
be upside to our full-year estimate of ~18.7K racks.
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): August revenue was  NT$460.1B (+49% m/m, +166% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) materially higher GB200/300 server computing
tray shipments  (2.3-2.4K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up approximately 100%

m/m), and higher Wiwynn revenue at ~NT$144.3B (+23% m/m, +50% y/y), while
offset by lower NB shipments at 1.4M units (-13% m/m, -36% y/y), lower DT
shipments at 500K (-29% m/m, -38% y/y) and lower monitor shipments at 750K (-
17% m/m, -6% y/y). Combined July + August revenue reached ~NT$768.3B, which is
74% of MSe and 75% of consensus 3Q revenue estimate, implying upside to 3Q. This
also implies that there could be upside to our full-year forecast of ~17.9K racks.

Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$921.8B (-3% m/m, +52% y/y), with
server-related revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by components
and other products, while computing products and consumer segment, which mainly
includes iPhone assembly, declined m/m. We model HH's GB rack shipments at 2.6K
racks (+6% m/m). Our full-year forecast is unchanged, at ~31.7K racks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in August at ~8.4K (+27% m/m), within
which:
Quanta shipped 2.5-2.55K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped 2.3-2.4K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.6K GB200/300 racks.
September 9, 2026 01:37 PM GMT

4
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Wistron Corporation (3231.TW)
Base case, residual income valuation. Key assumptions: 8.7% cost of equity, 7.0% medium-
term growth rate and 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected divestiture of consumer electronics business
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Margin expansion from better product mix

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Idea
Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
iPhone 18: Real Upgrade Cycle,
but Select Optical Valuations
Look Stretched
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific

Industry View In-Line
Exhibit                                                                              1                        :                    Summary of changes
Share prices as at: Sep 7, 2026.  Source: FactSet, Morgan Stanley Research
estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 10, 2026 08:01 AM GMT

Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives intrinsic value of NT$600/share for
the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related opportunities
could be valued at NT$310/share. Adding these together, we raise our price target

materially to NT$910, but that still implies 14% downside from the latest closing price.
3. Sunny: Apple revenue to double in 2026, but positive is
well known
We believe Sunny Optical will benefit from the new iPhone launch because:
1. The company is a key vendor of  variable aperture actuators – a key spec upgrade

### Page 8

M
Idea
8
with higher ASP.
2. Sunny is expanding the product mix from a single category to multiple categories,
which could generate higher revenue from the key client.
3. Sunny is also gaining share in high-end lens products.
These drivers collectively underpin management's guidance of doubling  Apple revenue for
the full-year of 2026.
However, the fundamental performance is in line with management's guidance, so there is
lack of potential positive surprise, in our view. We therefore raise our net income

estimates 29.6% for 2026 and 26.5% for 2027 based on upbeat 1H26 results.
Consequently, we raise our price target to HK$75 but maintain our EW rating –  the strong
growth from Apple has been well expected.
4. Crystal: Prisms, coated filters, and cover lenses to benefit
We expect Crystal Optech to benefit from Apple's new product launch because  the
company is supplying three key products in the supply chain:
1. Its micro prism modules are a key enabling component for periscope (folded-

optics) telephoto cameras in smartphones. The company's prism technology uses a
folded optical path to achieve long-focal-length telephoto capability without
increasing device thickness. In 1H26, the micro prism module business enjoyed
significant volume growth. The iPhone 18 launch will directly extend this demand
into 2H26.
2. Crystal-Optech's coated filter business — which brings micro-nano optical
processing into the IRCF manufacturing process to significantly improve petal

ghosting and chromatic aberration in special imaging scenarios — is another key
segment that benefits from the iPhone 18 launch. Apple's high-end Pro models are
the primary application for this product, so the company is highly likely to
generate rapid revenue and earnings growth.
3. The iPhone 18 Pro models are expected to feature advances in 3D sensing,
including a smaller Dynamic Island as some Face ID components move beneath the
display, which sustains demand for these optical components. Crystal Optech is

well positioned to capture the growth opportunity in iPhone's 3D sensing and
cover lenses.
The company's 2Q26 results were a bit weaker than we expected because of rising costs
from R&D and capacity expansion related to new products. However, we believe this
investment could lead to stronger growth in 2028 and onward. As a result, we trim our
earnings estimates 15.6% for 2026 and 2.9% for 2027 but raise our 2028 estimate 8.2%.
Consequently, we raise our price target  7%, to Rmb32, implying 24% upside from the

current level.
Luxshare: Apple opportunity underappreciated,  resilient
growth intact
We believe Luxshare's Apple opportunity is underappreciated –  some investors worry
about the supply chain relocation. However, the company is one of Apple's most deeply

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
embedded manufacturing partners, covering various products and components, as
illustrated in the chart below. The company has  also proved its operational resilience,
consistently registering positive growth in the Apple business.
Exhibit 7: Luxshare: One of the most deeply embedded manufacturing partners for Apple
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect Luxshare to benefit from the latest new product  launch through the following

four key drivers:
1.  Second largest assembler for iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max — the most premium-heavy
iPhone lineup in history. If the  Pro/Pro Max models can take 70%+ of total iPhone
volume, it could mark the highest high-end model contribution over the past
several model cycles. A higher share from high-end models implies better revenue
and margin outlook for Luxshare.
2.   Capturing component opportunity in new  variable aperture actuator supply.

Instead of doing assembly, Lxushare is also playing the role  of supplier of actuator
mechanisms for the variable aperture camera system in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro
Max. This helps Luxshare gain exposure to high-end component business, which
carries higher margin than assembly business.
3. Foldable iPhone (iPhone Ultra) exposure: While Luxshare is not the assembler for
the foldable phone, it has actively developed foldable hinge technology, with R&D

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
focused on high-precision, long-life, large-angle hinge modules for inward-folding
flexible-display devices. The company is also developing ultra-thin haptic motors
tailored for foldable smartphones. When foldable shipments reach a certain scale,
Luxshare is also highly likely to become the second assembler.
4.  Leading AirPods assembly share:  In the AirPods assembly market, we expect
Luxshare to remain the largest supplier, with GoerTek as the second player. The

iPhone 18 launch is accompanied by new AirPods models, and Luxshare's dominant
AirPods assembly position means that it captures the full breadth of Apple's 2H26
product cycle — not just the iPhone.
Despite a significant FX loss, Luxshare posted resilient earnings in 1H26. We nudge our
EPS estimates up 0.2% each for 2026 and 2027 and 2.0% for 2028 to reflect the solid
1H26 results and positive growth outlook in the following 2-3 years. As a result, we also

raise our H-share price target to HK$82/share, implying 34% upside potential.
Lingyi: Peak season to drive strong earnings improvement
Lingyi  provides a broad range of components, including core precision parts, hinge
precision structural components, screen support plates, flanged ultra-thin stainless steel
battery case modules, etc. Following the launch of iPhone 18 Pro, Pro Max, and Apple's
first-ever foldable iPhone, Lingyi is likely to benefit from  higher content per unit on

standard Pro models, and a step-change in content value from the foldable iPhone.
Exhibit 8: Lingyi: Higher content value from new iPhone
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
Lingyi also has good exposure to the iPhone  foldable product, with content value per
foldable device expected by the company to reach US$80–90, including titanium inner
hinges, hinge components, and mid-frame support structures — substantially above the
content value of conventional smartphones.

Considering the positive drivers in 3Q26, we expect Lingyi to achieve significant earnings
improvement on a QoQ basis, which could become a key re-rating catalyst.
We slightly adjust our net income  estimates after 1H26: up 1.6%  in 2026, 0.3% and 1.7% in
2027 and 2028. However, our price targets for both the H- and A-shares are unchanged
because the revision is minor while our long-term RI model assumptions remain

### Page 11

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 11
unchanged.
AAC: Key beneficiary of  iPhone VC application
AAC Tech is one of Apple's key component suppliers, providing a broad portfolio spanning
acoustics, haptics, and thermal management. The iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Ultra
launch is a direct, multi-dimensional catalyst across all those business segments.
Within the three segments, we believe thermal management –  Vapor Chamber (VC) Heat
Dissipation – is the most important catalyst. AAC Technologies is a key vendor of VC heat

spreaders in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max. The significant expansion of the VC cooling
area is driven by the iPhone 18 Pro's A20 Pro chip, which debuts on a 2nm process node
with complex WMCM packaging technology to support on-device AI computation, leading
to increased thermal output. Apple first introduced VC liquid cooling in the iPhone 17 Pro
series, with AAC as the primary supplier for that generation as well.
This division has already demonstrated significant growth momentum heading into the

iPhone 18 ramp up. In 1H26, heat dissipation revenue surged approximately 400% YoY, to
RMB1.10 billion, primarily driven by the continued ramp-up in shipments of high-
performance innovative vapor chambers. The company expects VC shipments to exceed
180,000k units in 2026, up from 140,000k+  units in 2025.

Combining the strong growth momentum in thermal management, but weaker-than-
expected performance in the optics segment (all supplied to Android), we raise our
earnings estimates  8.4% for 2026 and 6.0% for 2027. After 1H26 results, we believe
investors are aware of the weakness in optics, while the iPhone launch could help re-rate

the stock. We raise our price target to HK$50 – the stronger 19% increase reflects
earnings upgrades and a higher valuation for the thermal-management business – and
maintain our OW rating.
BYDE: Normal peak season, but lackluster with limited spec
upgrade

### Page 12

12
While Apple is BYDE's key customer, accounting for >50% of total revenue, it is mainly
from the  iPad assembly business. Therefore, this division is less likely to benefit from the
iPhone 18 launch.
The company is also the second-largest iPhone metal casing vendor, supplying precision
structural components including titanium and aluminium alloy chassis. While iPhone 18
launch is the direct driver for this segment, the titanium-to-aluminium downgrade has

become a near-term headwind.  Titanium is a higher-value, higher-margin material for BYDE
while aluminium carries lower ASP and lower gross margin. This change has made BYDE
less likely to benefit from the iPhone 18 launch.
Despite the lackluster outlook, we expect earnings to show sequential improvement in
3Q26, driven by the normal peak season in 3Q26, followed by stronger growth in 4Q26 as
liquid cooling products for AI infrastructure also ramp up. Our earnings estimates and

price target remain unchanged.
GoerTek: Margin might face pressures despite peak season
GoerTek's primary Apple exposure is in smart acoustic complete units (AirPods assembly
and components) and precision components. We believe the  iPhone 18 launch and peak
season ramp in 3Q–4Q will drive higher shipment volumes for these products.
However, GoerTek's transfer of production  to Vietnam — where it has invested
approximately US$1.3 billion through multiple entities — has created a persistent labor

cost headwind. In 2H25, a labor shortage caused by the rapid capacity transfer forced
GoerTek to offer higher wages, triggering material  gross margin pressure  for the smart
acoustic assembly business. Overall gross margins partially recovered to 13.8% in 1Q26
and 15.2% in 2Q26, but the underlying trend of rising wages in Vietnam remains a
structural factor. As GoerTek ramps production for the iPhone 18 peak season in 3Q and
4Q, the higher labor cost base in Vietnam will continue to weigh on assembly margins.

We raise our 2026 and 2027 earnings estimates 14.9% and 10.6%, respectively, mainly
reflecting the better-than-expected gross margin recovery in 1H26. However, we raise our
price target only modestly: 4%. to Rmb27, as persistent labor-cost pressure in Vietnam
could limit further margin expansion and reduce the earnings benefit from higher peak-
season volumes.
Considering the factors discussed above, we believe GoerTek is less likely to benefit from

the new iPhone product launch and peak season volume increase.

Where we could be wrong
While we expect iPhone 18 to be  a positive catalyst in 2H26, we are watching three key
variables on the potential inflection points:
1.  Memory prices: These are a key variable this year. If there is a memory price
correction anytime during 2026, investors are likely to expect an earlier
smartphone shipment and margin recovery, which could reverse the negative

sentiment.
2.  LLMs: We see LLMs as a revolutionary innovation. They could introduce numerous
creative features in smartphone applications and change user habits in the years to
come. If the Edge AI breakthrough arrives earlier than we expect, it could
accelerate the smartphone product cycle and make the recovery earlier.
3. iPhone sales performance: Better-than-expected sell-through of the iPhone 18
Pro, Pro Max and its first foldable model could drive shipment upside, higher

content value and stronger capacity utilization. Conversely, weaker demand could
lead to order cuts after the initial ramp up and reduce the earnings benefits from
key hardware upgrades.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

joey.t.yeh@fubon.com 
環宇-KY (4991 TT)

光收發模組帶動的成長爆發

初評給予買進，目標價 700 元，潛在上漲空間 42% 
1. 2Q26 營運受基板出口管制影響，毛利率略降 
2. 下半年基板狀況緩解，重回成長軌道 
3. 2027 年 100G PD 市占率回升，200G PD 為主要動能

2Q26 營運受基板出口管制影響，毛利率略降 
2Q26 營收 7.7 億元，季增8%，毛利率 43%，季減 12 個百分點主因 InP 基板限制
帶來的與原料短缺，壓抑 AOC 出貨占比降至 61%；另外存貨跌價損失與設備報廢
成本等影響也形成拖累。營業費用略增來自員工權利新股酬勞與研發投入增加，使
營益率降至 12.7%。業外部分貢獻 3553 萬元，主要來自轉投資事業上海宙鎵貢獻
近 2000 萬元，而常州承芯持股比例已經降至約 12%。綜上的單季稅後 EPS 0.92
元。

下半年基板狀況緩解，重回成長軌道 
環宇申請基板過程坎坷，五月底拿到中國商務部核准後又遇到颱風延誤，待上游供
應商做完送到美國廠，第一批需在八月才能交貨，略影響到第三季營運狀況，但此
次核准預期已足以支應下半年整體需求量至 1Q27。受惠出貨逐漸恢復正軌，公司
於法說會上指引營收將為 2,600 至 2,800 萬美元，毛利率落在 40%至 48%區間，
主要仍受到庫存中 RF 在製品的交貨履行、基板因素影響產品組合，尚沒辦法回到
1Q26 高點。綜上我們預期 3Q26 營收為 8.29 億元，季增 7.6%、年增 52%，毛利
率略增至 46.8%，單季 EPS 為 0.84 元。

2027 年 100G PD 市占率回升，200G PD 為主要動能 
環宇 2026 年因基板問題市占率受到影響，然 2027 年基板端的影響已逐漸可控，
最大供應商的申請狀況良好，其餘基板供應商也持續溝通以確保供應，我們預期
100G PD 市佔可望逐漸彌補自 2026 年因缺料而滑落的份額；200G PD 預計將於
3Q26 完成 2,000 小時燒機測試後進入量產 ，2027 將為首個完整放量年 ，200G PD
產品 ASP 為 100G PD 的 5 倍以上，將為 2027 年成長主要動能；公司亦積極開發
CW Laser，預期年底過燒機測試，今年小量出貨，為明年營運添增動能。綜上，我
們預期 2027 AOC 營收占比將為 88%，全年營收為 81.92 億元，年增129%，毛利
率為 51%，全年 EPS 為 19.08 元。

富邦不同之處 
富邦最新預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分為 5.69/19.08 元，高於 Bloomberg 
consensus 預估的 5.98/13.89 元，富邦認為光收發模組出貨支數放量帶動明顯帶
動營收增加。

評價與風險 
我們初評給予買進建議，目標價 700 元約為 2027 年預期 EPS 的 37 倍，潛在上漲
空間約 42%。我們看好環宇營運隨著光收發模組放量，AOC 產品將大幅成長，帶
動產品組合轉佳。參考現台灣三五族產業本益比倍數約在35-50 倍區間，我們認為
環宇 PD 短線因缺料面臨的市佔壓力會逐漸緩解，給予同族群區間偏中間之本益比
倍數。環宇主要的投資風險為，(1) 基板管制、(2) 市佔競爭。

### Page 2

台灣股市 | 半導體  
公司深入分析

請參閱末頁之免責聲明 2

公司簡介 – 代工廠轉型光通訊IDM廠 
代工廠轉型光通訊IDM廠 
環宇通訊半導體(環宇-KY)在 2010 年於美國加州成立，2012 年回台掛牌，主要從
事砷化鎵(GaAs)/磷化銦(InP)/氮化鎵(GaN)等三五族化合物半導體晶圓製造代工，
應用於射頻以及光電領域。

環宇-KY 營收依照業務別主要可分為： 
1) 射頻元件代工：主要為以砷化鎵(GaAs)/磷化銦(InP)/氮化鎵(GaN)等三五
族化合物晶圓為材料，進行射頻元件的代工製造，用於無線基地台、手機、國
防等領域之無線通訊，2021-2022 曾為公司最大的營收來源。

2)光電元件晶圓代工：主要為以砷化鎵(GaAs)/磷化銦(InP)晶圓為材料，代工
製造之光電元件，應用於光通訊、穿戴式裝置、醫學、Lidar 等領域。

3)自有光電元件 Known Good Die（KGD）：光電元件產品包含開發與生產
三五族化合物半導體之光探測器（PD/MPD）、雪崩式光探測器 （APD）、VCSEL、
雷射二極體（LD）等產品，應用於 100 Gbps-1.6Tbps 光通訊領域、Lidar 領
域等。

轉投資事業成為近年負擔，整理後將重新聚焦本業 
環宇-KY 過去以射頻與光通訊為雙營運主軸 ， 過去營運曾著重往中國大陸射頻廠區 、
第三代半導體等方向發展，然受到中美貿易戰的地緣政治：大陸 5G 產業建置進度
緩慢、疫情與中國去美化使光通訊需求放緩等影響，自 2020 年起明顯受轉投資事
業虧損拖累，2022/2023 年 EPS 更一度下探至-8.53 與-7.18 元。公司近年致力於
轉型為光通訊 IDM 廠，隨全球 800G、1.6T 光收發模組需求進入高成長期，光通訊
元件營收占比已成為現最大之業務。將進一步擴大；並陸續將 RF 部分製程 EOL，
篩選 RF 訂單也助於提高毛利率；最後公司將處分過去轉投資之大陸事業，將助於
減少業外影響數，將營運管理更集中回歸於本業。

圖表 2:歷年產品營收比重

資料來源：公司、富邦投顧整理

回顧環宇-KY 轉投資事業，由 2016 年三安光電擬併購環宇-KY 開始，卻遭美國外
商投資委員會(CFIUS)以產品涉及半導體技術轉移和軍事/國防安全疑慮為由阻擋，
欲改為成立合資公司亦於 2019 年撤銷。隨後轉向與晶電子公司常州晶品光電共同
成立新晶宇光電，2020 年更名為常州承芯半導體，主要從事無線射頻和光電 6 吋
晶圓代工。同年，環宇-KY 也與晶電（現富采投控）策略合作，取得旗下晶成半導
體 16.4%股權，亦為提供 6 吋晶圓代工服務。然於 2024 年，公司決議處分晶成半
導體股權予富采控股之子公司晶元光電，並取得微電子機器設備投入美國廠。

加上 2021 年為拓展光電元件生產而投資的上海宙鎵光電，截至 2025 年底，環宇-
KY 大陸投資事業僅存常州承芯半導體以及上海宙鎵光電，持股比例分別為 19.56%
以及 48.00%。常州承芯半導體過去長期處於虧損，公司積極降低持股比例至低於
20%，降低業外損失，1Q26 前兩個月仍有虧損認列，但 2Q26 開始就不會有虧損
認列。

廠房位於美國，RF產線將逐漸EOL 
環宇-KY 主要產能在美國，於 2025 年計劃在台灣新竹擴產，主要為 CW Laser 後
段產能，2025 年/2026 年資本支出分別約為 800 萬/2000 萬美金，除了用在美國
廠內產能轉換、設備汰舊換新，主要就是用於新竹廠。另外，2026 年 1 月環宇-KY
決議參與威力富 4,900 萬元的現金增資案，透過將威力富原有的 GaAs/GaN RF 舊
產能轉置為 InP 產線，產品將涵蓋 CW Laser 與 PD 產品。

為提升 PD 月產能，環宇-KY 同時也規劃 RF 代工產線的 EOL，將集中在低單價產
品，目標拉高 ASP 和提升高附加價值產品佔比，節制 2025 年中，已經有三個製程
EOL。公司給予客戶緩衝時間但也同時提高銷售價格。我們預期 RF 在未來可能會
剩下國防相關產品，在售價提升的情況下將有助於毛利率的改善。

上下游產業鏈 
三五族半導體 包含磷化銦(InP)與砷化鎵(GaAs)在光收發模組中具備不可替代的核
心優勢，主要在於其優良的發光特性，可用以製造核心的雷射晶片，其中磷化銦適
合製作長波長的光纖通訊如連續波 （CW） 大功率雷射器； 而砷化鎵則是短距離用的
VCSEL。針對光收發模組中的三五族化合物半導體上下游 ， 分別涵蓋從上游的基板 、
磊晶機台、磊晶廠，到中下游的代工廠和後段封裝測試，而國外大廠則多為垂直整
合的 IDM 形式。

圖表 5: 三五族化合物半導體產業鏈

產業概況 
PD的結構以及在光收發模組的角色 
雷射把電變成光，PD 則可理解成雷射的反向工程，把光變回電。PD 的本體是與一
般二極體連接方向相反的反向偏壓 PIN 結構，並在最外層有抗反射鍍膜。當光子進
入中間的吸收層後會打出電子，電場再把帶出電流，如此而已。一顆好的 PD 本質
上關注三個重點：1)光電轉換效率、2)漏電影響訊號乾淨程度、3)訊號快速閃爍的
反應速度。

PD 的與雷射相同 ， 仍然是依照材料特性決定 PD 適合光波長區段 ， 因光通訊主力波
長落在 1310 與 1550 nm ，目前長波長的主流材料仍是長在磷化銦（InP）基板上
的 InGaAs， 兩者的原子排列間距吻合 ， 長出來的晶體品質好 ，涵蓋通訊用的三個波
段，且轉換效率高、漏電低、訊號非常乾淨。SiGe 優勢則在能直接長在矽晶片的光
路上、與半導體標準製程相容，至於 850 nm 的短距多模場景之下則用 GaAs 系材
料。

圖表 6:PD 結構圖

資料來源：Enlitech

PD 是一顆獨立晶片，位在 ROSA（接收端次模組）的核心，前端連接透鏡/解多工
器將光纖來的光對準與依波長拆開；後面緊接 TIA，因為 PD 轉出的電流只有微安
等級，必須立刻放大成電壓，再交給DSP 還原資料送回晶片。PD 的主要功能就是
把光的強弱變化忠實還原成電流變化。

圖表 7:光收發模組結構圖

資料來源：QSFRTEK

光收發模組受到AI推動需求大增 
光收發模組是光通訊的核心，它們的主要功能是光電訊號轉換。這類產品主要由兩
部分組成：1) 包含雷射晶片的光發射器 （TOSA）；2)包含光電探測器晶片的接收器
（ROSA） 。 當發送端產生訊號時 ，TOSA 將電訊號轉換為光訊號 ， 並透過光纖傳輸 。
光訊號到達目的地後，ROSA 將其轉換回電訊號，已實現快速地訊號收發。相關技
術已成熟運用在電信和傳統資料中心等環境。然而，市場需求的爆炸性成長來自 AI
所帶來的全新運算架構。近年模型參數規模的不斷增長導致這些 AI 叢集中 GPU 之
間的資料交換量增加了百倍，這種成長速度遠遠超過了傳統資料中心網路頻寬的提
升。因此現在即使是微小的傳輸延遲也會導致整體 GPU 運作效能降低，因此資料
中 心 正 在 追 求 超 高 頻 寬 和 極 低 延 遲 的 結 合 。公 司 法 說 會 簡 報 指 出 ， 根 據
LightCounting ，2024-2029 年光收發模組市場 CAGR 為 21%，樂觀看待增長。

圖表 8:光收發市場 TAM                                            單位：百萬美元

資料來源：LightCounting、公司法說會簡報、富邦投顧整理

400G/800G轉向1.6T的軍備競賽 
在規格演進上 ， 光收發模組雖長期遵循傳輸速率翻倍 、 成本與功耗減半的摩爾定律 ，
但在 AI 快速發展之下，技術迭代週期已由傳統的四年強烈壓縮至兩年左右。目前
400G/800G 模組為 AI 資料中心的主流標配，其中 800G 產品在技術成熟後正處於
規模放量期。然而，面對算力需求的擴張，產業重心已迅速轉向具備更強低延遲特
性的 1.6T 規格 ，而 3.2T 技術亦已進入研究導入期 ， 成為下一個世代技術角力的關
鍵節點之一。富邦目前預估 2026/2027 的光收發模組總出貨支數將分別成長 109%
與 42.6%，將由 800G/1.6T 出貨的大幅成長所帶動。

成長動能 
100G 世代囊括主要市占率，200G世代為動能 
與大型光通 IDM 不同，環宇單純銷售 PD/LD 產品，而 IDM 通常銷售包含雷射、
Driver、DSP、TIA、PD 的套裝解決方案，故環宇可讓模組廠客戶搭配其自研或是
購買的雷射。2025 年環宇共出貨 1.7 億顆 PD，以市場共識 800G 光收發模組出貨
支數 2200-2500 萬支來看，環宇在 100G/Lane PD 的市場份額佔近七成，其餘則
為 Broadcom、MACOM 等，其中 Broadcom 又為 IDM，因此絕大多數光收發模
組廠都是環宇的客戶。

圖表 11:100G/Lane PD 的市佔狀況

資料來源：富邦投顧預估

我們認為進到 200G/Lane 世代，環宇能延續其高市佔狀況，因1) 未來產能來自業
務組合切換、而非新建產線，擴產不受設備交期、廠房建置影響；2) 市場供應結構
上 ， 當單純CW Laser 供應商滲透率提高代表環宇 PD 的可及市場就愈大；3) 在 InP
基板與磊晶產能全面吃緊的環境下，任何一家雷射廠的排序考量上會將自有產線保
留給雷射端而 PD 產品則會外找居多。根據調查，200G/Lane PD 的單顆 ASP 為
100G/Lane PD 的五倍以上 。 因此隨著1.6T 產品放量 ， 我們認為公司營收獲利將顯
著成長。

積極切入發射端，為另一成長助力 
從光收發模組出貨支數成長速度來看，可預期發射端 CW Laser 的需求量也將同步
大幅增長，而供給端僅有 Sumitomo、源傑、聯亞等三家，供需缺口浮現替環宇帶
來另一個成長動能。環宇過去引進合作夥伴的 70mW CW Laser 技術，以此為基準
向上開發 100mW，目前已在模組端進行可靠度測試。今年將小量試產，明後年出
貨量預期將有倍數提升，公司也持續開發 150mW 以後之規格。

營運狀況 
2Q26營運受基板出口管制影響，毛利率略降 
2Q26 營收 7.7 億元，季增 8%、年增 57%，毛利率 43%，季減 12 個百分點、年
減 6 個百分點，主因 InP 基板限制帶來的與原料短缺，壓抑 AOC 出貨占比自上季
的 74%降至 61%，毛利較低的 RF 代工占比由 12%升至 23%；另外存貨跌價損失
與設備報廢成本等影響也形成拖累。營業費用略增來自員工權利新股酬勞與研發投
入增加，使營益率降至 12.7%。業外部分貢獻 3553 萬元，除了利息收入之外，主
要來自轉投資事業上海 宙鎵貢獻近 2000 萬元，而常州承芯持股比例已經降至約
12%。 另外公司財報中威力富科技自今年 4 月起已納入合併 ， 公司目前取得 60.05% 
股權；以 2Q26 來看貢獻營收 835 萬元、稅前淨損 1617 萬元，可見仍處建置期而
未進入貢獻階段，綜上的單季稅後 EPS 0.92 元。

下半年基板狀況緩解，重回成長軌道 
環宇申請基板過程坎坷，五月底拿到中國商務部核准後又遇到颱風延誤，待上游供
應商做完送到美國廠，第一批需在八月才能交貨，略影響到第三季營運狀況，但此
次核准預期已足以支應下半年整體需求量至 1Q27。受惠出貨逐漸恢復正軌，公司
於法說會上指引營收將為 2,600 至 2,800 萬美元，毛利率落在 40%至 48%區間，
主要仍受到庫存中 RF 在製品的交貨履行、基板因素影響產品組合，尚沒辦法回到
1Q26 高點。綜上我們預期 3Q26 營收為 8.29 億元，季增 7.6%、年增 52%，毛利
率略增至 46.8%，單季 EPS 為 0.84 元。

2027 100G PD市占率回升，200G主要動能 
受到基板供應影響，環宇 2026 全年 100G PD 市占率受到影響，然 2027 年基板端
的影響已逐漸可控，最大供應商的申請狀況良好，其餘日系、歐系基板供應商也持
續溝通以確保供應 ， 公司認為InP PD 在靈敏度和整體經濟性上仍優於 SiGe 解決方
案，預期 100G PD 市佔可望逐漸彌補自 2026 年因缺料而滑落的份額；200G PD
預計將於 3Q26 完成 2,000 小時燒機測試後進入量產 ，預期 2027 為首個完整放量
年，200G PD 產品 ASP 為 100G PD 的 5 倍以上，將為 2027 年成長主要動能；公
司亦積極開發 CW Laser，預期年底過燒機測試，今年小量出貨，為明年營運添增動
能。綜上，我們預期 2027 AOC 營收占比將為 88%，全年營收為 81.92 億元，年增
129%，毛利率為 51%，全年 EPS 為 19.08 元。

評價 
我們初評給予買進建議，目標價 700 元約為 2027 年預期 EPS 的 37 倍，潛在上漲
空間約 42%。我們看好環宇在 100G/200G PD 的產品對應到 800G/1.6T 光收發模
組上，隨著光收發模組放量，將帶動 AOC 產品大幅成長，營收佔比提升也將帶動
產品組合轉佳。我們認為憑藉 InP 物理特性以及過去環宇出貨實績，環宇可維持
100G PD 高市占率，同時將逐步拓展 200G PD 市占率，CW Laser 則是額外添加
成長動能 。 現台灣三五族產業本益比倍數約在35-50 倍區間 ， 差異在於光通訊佔比 、
獨佔優勢等，我們認為環宇 PD 短線因缺料面臨的市佔壓力會逐漸緩解，給予同族
群區間中間的本益比倍數。環宇 主要的投資風險為，(1) 基板管制，上游基板最大
供應商仍面臨來自中國商務部的出口管制 ， 雖現在基板狀況無虞 ， 但管控風險仍在 、
(2) 市佔競爭，上有 AVGO、MTSI 等強力對手，下有 SiGe PD，具備成本，製程契
合度等優勢。
